```
    34→# Finding-ID citation extraction
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→FINDING_ID_RE = re.compile(r"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}")
    38→
    39→
    40→def extract_finding_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    41→    """Extract finding IDs cited in free text.
    42→
    43→    Matches full finding IDs (e.g. ``review::abcdef12``) or bare 8+ char
    44→    hex suffixes that correspond to a known finding.
    45→    """
    46→    cited: set[str] = set()
    47→    # Match full finding IDs
    48→    for match in FINDING_ID_RE.finditer(text):
    49→        candidate = match.group()
    50→        if candidate in valid_ids:
    51→            cited.add(candidate)
    52→    # Match 8+ char hex suffixes
    53→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", text):
    54→        for valid_id in valid_ids:
    55→            if valid_id.endswith("::" + token):
    56→                cited.add(valid_id)
    57→                break
    58→    return cited
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# Completed-work helpers
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    66→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    67→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    68→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    69→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    70→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    71→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    72→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    73→                if ts:
    74→                    return str(ts)
    75→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    76→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    77→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    78→    return None
    79→
    80→
    81→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    82→    open_findings: dict[str, dict],
    83→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict],
    84→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    85→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open findings.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    88→    A dimension with both resolved and open findings signals a potential
    89→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    90→    """
    91→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    92→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    93→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    94→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    95→        return ""
    96→
    97→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    98→    for fid, f in open_findings.items():
    99→        dim = _dimension(f)
   100→        if dim:
   101→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   102→
   103→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   104→    for fid, f in resolved_findings.items():
   105→        dim = _dimension(f)
   106→        if dim:
   107→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   108→
   109→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
   110→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
   111→        recurring[dim] = {
   112→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
   113→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
   114→        }
   115→    return recurring
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Data types
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→@dataclass
   123→class SynthesisInput:
   124→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
   125→
   126→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
   127→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
   128→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters to update
   129→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
   130→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
   131→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
   132→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
   133→    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
   134→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict]   # full finding objects for resolved IDs
   135→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last synthesis
   136→
   137→
   138→@dataclass
   139→class DismissedFinding:
   140→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
   141→
   142→    finding_id: str
   143→    reason: str
   144→
   145→
   146→@dataclass
   147→class ContradictionNote:
   148→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
   149→
   150→    kept: str
   151→    dismissed: str
   152→    reason: str
   153→
   154→
   155→@dataclass
   156→class SynthesisResult:
   157→    """Parsed and validated LLM synthesis output."""
   158→
   159→    strategy_summary: str
   160→    epics: list[dict]
   161→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
   162→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
   163→    priority_rationale: str = ""
   164→
   165→
   166→@dataclass
   167→class SynthesisMutationResult:
   168→    """What changed when synthesis was applied to the plan."""
   169→
   170→    epics_created: int = 0
   171→    epics_updated: int = 0
   172→    epics_completed: int = 0
   173→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
   174→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
   175→    strategy_summary: str = ""
   176→    synthesis_version: int = 0
   177→    dry_run: bool = False
   178→
   179→
   180→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→# Input collection
   182→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→
   184→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
   185→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
   186→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   187→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   188→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   189→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   190→
   191→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   192→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   193→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   194→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   195→            continue
   196→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   197→            open_review[fid] = f
   198→        else:
   199→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   200→
   201→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   202→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   203→    new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
   204→    resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids
   205→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   206→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   207→
   208→    # Resolved finding objects (for REFLECT stage)
   209→    resolved_finding_objs = {
   210→        fid: findings[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in findings
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    # Completed clusters since last synthesis completion
   214→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   215→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   216→    if last_completed:
   217→        recent_completed = [
   218→            c for c in all_completed
   219→            if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   220→        ]
   221→    else:
   222→        recent_completed = list(all_completed)
   223→
   224→    return SynthesisInput(
   225→        open_findings=open_review,
   226→        mechanical_findings=open_mechanical,
   227→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   228→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   229→        new_since_last=new_since,
   230→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   231→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   232→        synthesis_version=version,
   233→        resolved_findings=resolved_finding_objs,
   234→        completed_clusters=recent_completed,
   235→    )
   236→
   237→
   238→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   239→# Prompt construction
   240→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   241→
   242→_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   243→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   244→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.
   245→
   246→Your plan should:
   247→- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   248→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   249→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   250→- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   251→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   252→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   253→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently
   254→
   255→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   256→
   257→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   258→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   259→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   260→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   261→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   262→- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   263→- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   264→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   265→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   266→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings
   267→
   268→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
   269→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
   270→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   271→
   272→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   273→{
   274→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   275→  "epics": [
   276→    {
   277→      "name": "slug-name",
   278→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   279→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   280→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   281→      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   282→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   283→      "agent_safe": true,
   284→      "dependency_order": 1,
   285→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   286→      "status": "pending"
   287→    }
   288→  ],
   289→  "dismissed_findings": [
   290→    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}
   291→  ],
   292→  "contradiction_notes": [
   293→    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
   294→  ],
   295→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   296→}
   297→"""
   298→
   299→
   300→def build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str:
   301→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all finding data."""
   302→    parts: list[str] = []
   303→
   304→    # Section: existing epics
   305→    if si.existing_epics:
   306→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   307→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   308→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   309→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   310→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   311→            fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   312→            parts.append(
   313→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   314→                f"\n  Findings: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   315→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   316→            )
   317→        parts.append("")
   318→
   319→    # Section: what changed
   320→    if si.new_since_last:
   321→        parts.append(f"## New findings since last synthesis ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   322→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   323→            f = si.open_findings.get(fid, {})
   324→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   325→        parts.append("")
   326→
   327→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   328→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last synthesis ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   329→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   330→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   331→        parts.append("")
   332→
   333→    # Section: all open review findings
   334→    parts.append(f"## All open review findings ({len(si.open_findings)})")
   335→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_findings.items()):
   336→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   337→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   338→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   339→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   340→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   341→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   342→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   343→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   344→        if dimension:
   345→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   346→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   347→        if suggestion:
   348→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   349→    parts.append("")
   350→
   351→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   352→    if si.dimension_scores:
   353→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   354→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   355→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   356→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   357→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   358→                issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   359→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   360→        parts.append("")
   361→
   362→    # Section: previously dismissed
   363→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   364→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   365→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   366→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   367→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   368→        parts.append("")
   369→
   370→    return "\n".join(parts)
   371→
   372→
   373→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   374→# Result parsing
   375→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   376→
   377→def parse_synthesis_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> SynthesisResult:
   378→    """Parse and validate raw LLM output into a SynthesisResult.
   379→
   380→    Invalid finding IDs are silently dropped from epics and dismissals.
   381→    """
   382→    strategy_summary = str(raw.get("strategy_summary", ""))
   383→
   384→    epics: list[dict] = []
   385→    for raw_epic in raw.get("epics", []):
   386→        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):
   387→            continue
   388→        name = str(raw_epic.get("name", "")).strip()
   389→        if not name:
   390→            continue
   391→        # Validate direction
   392→        direction = str(raw_epic.get("direction", "simplify")).strip()
   393→        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:
   394→            direction = "simplify"
   395→        # Filter to valid finding IDs
   396→        finding_ids = [
   397→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   398→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   399→        ]
   400→        dismissed = [
   401→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("dismissed", [])
   402→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   403→        ]
   404→        action_steps = [
   405→            str(s) for s in raw_epic.get("action_steps", [])
   406→            if isinstance(s, str)
   407→        ]
   408→
   409→        epics.append({
   410→            "name": name,
   411→            "thesis": str(raw_epic.get("thesis", "")),
   412→            "direction": direction,
   413→            "root_cause": str(raw_epic.get("root_cause", "")),
   414→            "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   415→            "dismissed": dismissed,
   416→            "agent_safe": bool(raw_epic.get("agent_safe", False)),
   417→            "dependency_order": int(raw_epic.get("dependency_order", 999)),
   418→            "action_steps": action_steps,
   419→            "status": str(raw_epic.get("status", "pending")),
   420→        })
   421→
   422→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = []
   423→    for d in raw.get("dismissed_findings", []):
   424→        if not isinstance(d, dict):
   425→            continue
   426→        fid = str(d.get("finding_id", ""))
   427→        if fid in valid_ids:
   428→            dismissed_findings.append(
   429→                DismissedFinding(finding_id=fid, reason=str(d.get("reason", "")))
   430→            )
   431→
   432→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []
   433→    for c in raw.get("contradiction_notes", []):
   434→        if not isinstance(c, dict):
   435→            continue
   436→        contradiction_notes.append(ContradictionNote(
   437→            kept=str(c.get("kept", "")),
   438→            dismissed=str(c.get("dismissed", "")),
   439→            reason=str(c.get("reason", "")),
   440→        ))
   441→
   442→    priority_rationale = str(raw.get("priority_rationale", ""))
   443→
   444→    return SynthesisResult(
   445→        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,
   446→        epics=epics,
   447→        dismissed_findings=dismissed_findings,
   448→        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,
   449→        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,
   450→    )
   451→
   452→
   453→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   454→# Plan mutation
   455→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   456→
   457→def apply_synthesis_to_plan(
   458→    plan: PlanModel,
   459→    state: StateModel,
   460→    synthesis: SynthesisResult,
   461→    *,
   462→    trigger: str = "manual",
   463→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   464→    """Apply parsed synthesis result to the living plan.
   465→
   466→    1. Creates/updates synthesis-clusters in plan["clusters"]
   467→    2. Marks dismissed findings as synthesized_out skips
   468→    3. Reorders queue_order to group epic members by dependency_order
   469→    4. Updates epic_synthesis_meta with snapshot hash
   470→    """
   471→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   472→    now = utc_now()
   473→    result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   474→    result.strategy_summary = synthesis.strategy_summary
   475→
   476→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   477→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
   478→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   479→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   480→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   481→    result.synthesis_version = version
   482→
   483→    # --- Update/create synthesis-clusters ------------------------------------
   484→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   485→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   486→        epic_name = f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}" if not raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else raw_name
   487→
   488→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   489→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   490→            # Update existing synthesis-cluster
   491→            existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   492→            existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
   493→            existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
   494→            existing["finding_ids"] = epic_data["finding_ids"]
   495→            existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
   496→            existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
   497→            existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
   498→            existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
   499→            existing["updated_at"] = now
   500→            existing["synthesis_version"] = version
   501→            existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   502→            # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
   503→            if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
   504→                existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
   505→            result.epics_updated += 1
   506→        else:
   507→            # Create new synthesis-cluster
   508→            cluster: Cluster = {
   509→                "name": epic_name,
   510→                "description": epic_data["thesis"],
   511→                "finding_ids": epic_data["finding_ids"],
   512→                "auto": True,
   513→                "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
   514→                "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   515→                "user_modified": False,
   516→                "created_at": now,
   517→                "updated_at": now,
   518→                # Epic fields
   519→                "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
   520→                "direction": epic_data["direction"],
   521→                "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
   522→                "supersedes": [],
   523→                "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
   524→                "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   525→                "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   526→                "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   527→                "source_clusters": [],
   528→                "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   529→                "synthesis_version": version,
   530→            }
   531→            clusters[epic_name] = cluster
   532→            result.epics_created += 1
   533→
   534→    # --- Dismiss findings ---------------------------------------------------
   535→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
   536→    for df in synthesis.dismissed_findings:
   537→        fid = df.finding_id
   538→        dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   539→        # Remove from queue, add to skipped as synthesized_out
   540→        if fid in order:
   541→            order.remove(fid)
   542→        skipped[fid] = {
   543→            "finding_id": fid,
   544→            "kind": "synthesized_out",
   545→            "reason": df.reason,
   546→            "note": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   547→            "attestation": None,
   548→            "created_at": now,
   549→            "review_after": None,
   550→            "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   551→        }
   552→        result.findings_dismissed += 1
   553→
   554→    # Also handle per-epic dismissed lists
   555→    for epic_data in synthesis.epics:
   556→        for fid in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
   557→            if fid not in dismissed_ids and fid in order:
   558→                order.remove(fid)
   559→                dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   560→                skipped[fid] = {
   561→                    "finding_id": fid,
   562→                    "kind": "synthesized_out",
   563→                    "reason": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   564→                    "note": None,
   565→                    "attestation": None,
   566→                    "created_at": now,
   567→                    "review_after": None,
   568→                    "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   569→                }
   570→                result.findings_dismissed += 1
   571→
   572→    # --- Reorder queue: epic findings grouped by dependency_order -----------
   573→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   574→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   575→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   576→        for fid in epic_data["finding_ids"]:
   577→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids and fid not in dismissed_ids:
   578→                epic_finding_ids.add(fid)
   579→                epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   580→
   581→    # Rebuild order: epic items first (by dependency), then non-epic items in original order
   582→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_finding_ids]
   583→    # Insert epic items at the front (after synthesis::pending if present)
   584→    new_order: list[str] = []
   585→    # Keep synthesis::pending at front if present (it will be pruned next sync)
   586→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in non_epic_items:
   587→        non_epic_items.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   588→    new_order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   589→    new_order.extend(non_epic_items)
   590→    order.clear()
   591→    order.extend(new_order)
   592→
   593→    # --- Update synthesis meta -----------------------------------------------
   594→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   595→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   596→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   597→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   598→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   599→    )
   600→
   601→    plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {
   602→        "synthesized_ids": open_review_ids,
   603→        "last_run": now,
   604→        "version": version,
   605→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   606→        "finding_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   607→        "strategy_summary": synthesis.strategy_summary,
   608→        "trigger": trigger,
   609→    }
   610→    plan["updated"] = now
   611→
   612→    return result
   613→
   614→
   615→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   616→# Top-level orchestrator
   617→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   618→
   619→@dataclass
   620→class SynthesisDeps:
   621→    """Injectable dependencies for the synthesis engine."""
   622→
   623→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
   624→
   625→
   626→def synthesize_epics(
   627→    plan: PlanModel,
   628→    state: StateModel,
   629→    *,
   630→    deps: SynthesisDeps | None = None,
   631→    dry_run: bool = False,
   632→    trigger: str = "manual",
   633→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   634→    """Run epic synthesis: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   635→
   636→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   637→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   638→
   639→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   640→    """
   641→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   642→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   643→
   644→    prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
   645→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   646→
   647→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   648→        result = SynthesisMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   649→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_findings)} findings"
   650→        return result
   651→
   652→    # Call LLM
   653→    try:
   654→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   655→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   656→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   657→        logger.error("Epic synthesis LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   658→        result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   659→        result.strategy_summary = f"Synthesis failed: {exc}"
   660→        return result
   661→
   662→    synthesis = parse_synthesis_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   663→    return apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, trigger=trigger)
   664→
   665→
   666→__all__ = [
   667→    "DismissedFinding",
   668→    "FINDING_ID_RE",
   669→    "SynthesisDeps",
   670→    "SynthesisInput",
   671→    "SynthesisMutationResult",
   672→    "SynthesisResult",
   673→    "apply_synthesis_to_plan",
   674→    "build_synthesis_prompt",
   675→    "collect_synthesis_input",
   676→    "detect_recurring_patterns",
   677→    "extract_finding_citations",
   678→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   679→    "parse_synthesis_result",
   680→    "synthesize_epics",
   681→]
   682→
```

```
    76→
    77→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    78→    """Return a banner string when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue."""
    79→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    80→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    81→        return ""
    82→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    83→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    84→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
    85→    if completed:
    86→        return (
    87→            f"Synthesis in progress ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete). "
    88→            "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    89→        )
    90→    return (
    91→        "Synthesis needed — review findings require analysis before fixing. "
    92→        "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→__all__ = [
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    10→    cmd_plan_describe,
    11→    cmd_plan_done,
    12→    cmd_plan_focus,
    13→    cmd_plan_note,
    14→    cmd_plan_reopen,
    15→    cmd_plan_skip,
    16→    cmd_plan_unskip,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
    19→from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output as _plan_output_impl
    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, reset_plan, save_plan
    22→
    23→
    24→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    25→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    26→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    27→
    28→
    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    31→    plan = load_plan()
    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    36→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    reset_plan(plan)
    74→    save_plan(plan)
    75→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    76→
    77→
    78→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    79→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    80→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    81→
    82→    if plan_action is None:
    83→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action == "show":
    87→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    88→        return
    89→
    90→    if plan_action == "queue":
    91→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    92→        return
    93→
    94→    if plan_action == "reset":
    95→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    96→        return
    97→
    98→    if plan_action == "move":
    99→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    if plan_action in (
   103→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   104→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   105→    ):
   106→        dispatch = {
   107→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   108→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   109→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   110→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   111→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   112→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   113→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   114→        }
   115→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   119→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   123→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   124→
   125→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   129→
   130→
   131→# Backwards-compatible alias
   132→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   133→
   134→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   135→
```

```
    10→
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)
    20→
    21→
    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)
    27→        kwargs.setdefault("const", True)
    28→        kwargs.setdefault("default", False)
    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

   214→        "--runner",
   215→        choices=["codex"],
   216→        default="codex",
   217→        help="Subagent runner backend (default: codex)",
   218→    )
   219→    g_batch.add_argument(
   220→        "--parallel", action="store_true", help="Run selected batches in parallel"
   221→    )
   222→    g_batch.add_argument(
   223→        "--max-parallel-batches",
   224→        type=int,
   225→        default=3,
   226→        help=(
   227→            "Max concurrent subagent batches when --parallel is enabled "
   228→            "(default: 3)"
   229→        ),
   230→    )
   231→    g_batch.add_argument(
   232→        "--batch-timeout-seconds",
   233→        type=int,
   234→        default=20 * 60,
   235→        help="Per-batch runner timeout in seconds (default: 1200)",
   236→    )
   237→    g_batch.add_argument(
   238→        "--batch-max-retries",
   239→        type=int,
   240→        default=1,
   241→        help=(
   242→            "Retries per failed batch for transient runner/network errors "
   243→            "(default: 1)"
   244→        ),
   245→    )
   246→    g_batch.add_argument(
   247→        "--batch-retry-backoff-seconds",
   248→        type=float,
   249→        default=2.0,
   250→        help=(
   251→            "Base backoff delay for transient batch retries in seconds "
   252→            "(default: 2.0)"
   253→        ),
   254→    )
   255→    g_batch.add_argument(
   256→        "--batch-heartbeat-seconds",
   257→        type=float,
   258→        default=15.0,
   259→        help=(
   260→            "Progress heartbeat interval during parallel batch runs in seconds "
   261→            "(default: 15.0)"
   262→        ),
   263→    )
   264→    g_batch.add_argument(
   265→        "--batch-stall-warning-seconds",
   266→        type=int,
   267→        default=0,
   268→        help=(
   269→            "Emit warning when a running batch exceeds this elapsed time "
   270→            "(0 disables warnings; does not terminate the batch)"
   271→        ),
   272→    )
   273→    g_batch.add_argument(
   274→        "--batch-stall-kill-seconds",
   275→        type=int,
   276→        default=120,
   277→        help=(
   278→            "Terminate a batch when output state is unchanged and runner streams are idle "
   279→            "for this many seconds (default: 120; 0 disables kill recovery)"
   280→        ),
   281→    )
   282→    g_batch.add_argument(
   283→        "--dry-run",
   284→        action="store_true",
   285→        help="Generate packet/prompts only (skip runner/import)",
   286→    )
   287→    g_batch.add_argument(
   288→        "--run-log-file",
   289→        type=str,
   290→        default=None,
   291→        help=(
   292→            "Optional explicit path for live run log output "
   293→            "(overrides default run artifacts path)"
   294→        ),
   295→    )
   296→    g_batch.add_argument(
   297→        "--packet",
   298→        type=str,
   299→        default=None,
   300→        help="Use an existing immutable packet JSON instead of preparing a new one",
   301→    )
   302→    g_batch.add_argument(
   303→        "--only-batches",
   304→        type=str,
   305→        default=None,
   306→        help="Comma-separated 1-based batch indexes to run (e.g. 1,3,5)",
   307→    )
   308→    g_batch.add_argument(
   309→        "--scan-after-import",
   310→        action="store_true",
   311→        help="Run `scan` after successful merged import",
   312→    )
   313→    g_batch.add_argument(
   314→        "--import-run",
   315→        dest="import_run_dir",
   316→        type=str,
   317→        metavar="DIR",
   318→        default=None,
   319→        help=(
   320→            "Re-import results from a completed run directory "
   321→            "(replays merge+import when the original pipeline was interrupted)"
   322→        ),
   323→    )
   324→
   325→    # -- trust & attestation --
   326→    g_trust = p_review.add_argument_group("trust & attestation")
   327→    g_trust.add_argument(
   328→        "--manual-override",
   329→        action="store_true",
   330→        help=(
   331→            "Allow untrusted assessment score imports. Findings always import; "
   332→            "scores require trusted blind provenance unless this override is set."
   333→        ),
   334→    )
   335→    g_trust.add_argument(
   336→        "--attested-external",
   337→        action="store_true",
   338→        help=(
   339→            "Accept external blind-run assessments as durable scores when "
   340→            "paired with --attest and valid blind packet provenance "
   341→            "(intended for cloud Claude subagent workflows)."
   342→        ),
   343→    )
   344→    g_trust.add_argument(
   345→        "--attest",
   346→        type=str,
   347→        default=None,
   348→        help=(
   349→            "Required with --manual-override or --attested-external. "
   350→            "For attested external imports include both phrases "
   351→            "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'."
   352→        ),
   353→    )
   354→
   355→    # -- post-processing --
   356→    g_post = p_review.add_argument_group("post-processing")
   357→    g_post.add_argument(
   358→        "--merge",
   359→        action="store_true",
   360→        help="Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings",
   361→    )
   362→    g_post.add_argument(
   363→        "--similarity",
   364→        type=float,
   365→        default=0.8,
   366→        help="Summary similarity threshold for merge (0-1, default: 0.8)",
   367→    )
   368→
   369→    # -- deprecated --
   370→    g_deprecated = p_review.add_argument_group("deprecated")
   371→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   372→        "--max-age",
   373→        type=int,
   374→        default=None,
   375→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   376→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   377→    )
   378→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   379→        "--max-files",
   380→        type=int,
   381→        default=None,
   382→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   383→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   384→    )
   385→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   386→        "--refresh",
   387→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   388→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   389→    )
   390→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   391→        "--holistic",
   392→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   393→        help="Deprecated: holistic is now the only review mode",
   394→    )
   395→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   396→        "--save-run-log",
   397→        action="store_true",
   398→        help=(
   399→            "Deprecated no-op: run logs are now always saved while running "
   400→            "(default location: run artifacts dir)"
   401→        ),
   402→    )

   405→def _add_zone_parser(sub) -> None:
   406→    p_zone = sub.add_parser("zone", help="Show/set/clear zone classifications")
   407→    p_zone.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   408→    p_zone.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   409→    zone_sub = p_zone.add_subparsers(dest="zone_action")
   410→    zone_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show zone classifications for all files")
   411→    z_set = zone_sub.add_parser("set", help="Override zone for a file")
   412→    z_set.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   413→    z_set.add_argument(
   414→        "zone_value",
   415→        type=str,
   416→        help="Zone (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)",
   417→    )
   418→    z_clear = zone_sub.add_parser("clear", help="Remove zone override for a file")
   419→    z_clear.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   420→

   422→def _add_config_parser(sub) -> None:

   424→    config_sub = p_config.add_subparsers(dest="config_action")

   426→    c_set = config_sub.add_parser("set", help="Set a config value")

   433→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   434→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   435→    for lang_name in langs:
   436→        try:
   437→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   438→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   439→            fixer_names = []
   440→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   441→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   442→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   443→    return fixer_help_lines
   444→
   445→
   446→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   447→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   448→        "fix",
   449→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   450→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   451→    )
   452→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   453→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   454→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   455→    p_fix.add_argument(
   456→        "--dry-run",
   457→        action="store_true",
   458→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   459→    )
   460→

   462→def _add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   463→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   464→        "plan",
   465→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
   466→        description="""\
   467→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
   468→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
   469→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
   470→        epilog="""\
   471→typical workflow:
   472→  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
   473→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
   474→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
   475→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
   476→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
   477→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
   478→  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
   479→
   480→subcommands:
   481→  show       Show plan metadata summary
   482→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
   483→  reset      Reset plan to empty
   484→  move       Move findings in the queue
   485→  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
   486→  describe   Set augmented description
   487→  note       Set note on findings
   488→  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
   489→  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
   490→  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
   491→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
   492→  cluster    Manage finding clusters""",
   493→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   494→    )
   495→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   496→    p_plan.add_argument(
   497→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE", help="Write to file instead of stdout"
   498→    )
   499→
   500→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
   501→
   502→    # plan show
   503→    plan_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show plan metadata summary")
   504→
   505→    # plan queue
   506→    p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
   507→    p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
   508→    p_queue.add_argument("--tier", type=int, choices=[1, 2, 3, 4], default=None,
   509→                         help="Show only items in this tier (T1=critical .. T4=cosmetic)")
   510→    p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
   511→                         help="Filter to a specific cluster")
   512→    p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
   513→                         help="Include skipped items at end")
   514→
   515→    # plan reset
   516→    plan_sub.add_parser("reset", help="Reset plan to empty")
   517→
   518→    # plan move <patterns> <position> [target|N]
   519→    p_move = plan_sub.add_parser(
   520→        "move",
   521→        help="Move findings in the queue",
   522→        epilog="""\
   523→examples:
   524→  desloppify plan move security top                    # prioritize a detector
   525→  desloppify plan move "unused::src/foo.ts::*" top     # prioritize by pattern
   526→  desloppify plan move smells bottom                   # deprioritize smells
   527→  desloppify plan move unused before security          # move before another""",
   528→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   529→    )
   530→    p_move.add_argument(
   531→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   532→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   533→    )
   534→    p_move.add_argument(
   535→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
   536→        help="Where to move (top, bottom, before, after, up N, down N)",
   537→    )
   538→    p_move.add_argument(
   539→        "target", nargs="?", default=None,
   540→        help="Target finding ID (for before/after) or offset (for up/down)",
   541→    )
   542→
   543→    # plan describe <patterns> "<text>"
   544→    p_describe = plan_sub.add_parser("describe", help="Set augmented description")
   545→    p_describe.add_argument(
   546→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   547→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   548→    )
   549→    p_describe.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Description text")
   550→
   551→    # plan note <patterns> "<text>"
   552→    p_note = plan_sub.add_parser("note", help="Set note on findings")
   553→    p_note.add_argument(
   554→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   555→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   556→    )
   557→    p_note.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Note text")
   558→
   559→    # plan skip <patterns> [--reason] [--review-after N] [--permanent] [--false-positive] [--note] [--attest]
   560→    p_skip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   561→        "skip",
   562→        help="Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent (wontfix), or --false-positive",
   563→    )
   564→    p_skip.add_argument(
   565→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   566→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   567→    )
   568→    p_skip.add_argument("--reason", type=str, default=None, help="Why this is being skipped")
   569→    p_skip.add_argument(
   570→        "--review-after", type=int, default=None, metavar="N",
   571→        help="Re-surface after N scans (temporary only)",
   572→    )
   573→    p_skip.add_argument(
   574→        "--permanent", action="store_true",
   575→        help="Mark as wontfix (score-affecting, requires --note and --attest)",
   576→    )
   577→    p_skip.add_argument(
   578→        "--false-positive", action="store_true",
   579→        help="Mark as false positive (requires --attest)",
   580→    )
   581→    p_skip.add_argument("--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation (required for --permanent)")
   582→    p_skip.add_argument(
   583→        "--attest", type=str, default=None,
   584→        help="Attestation (required for --permanent and --false-positive)",
   585→    )
   586→
   587→    # plan unskip <patterns>
   588→    p_unskip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   589→        "unskip", help="Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens permanent/fp in state)"
   590→    )
   591→    p_unskip.add_argument(
   592→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   593→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   594→    )
   595→
   596→    # plan reopen <patterns>
   597→    p_reopen = plan_sub.add_parser(
   598→        "reopen", help="Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue"
   599→    )
   600→    p_reopen.add_argument(
   601→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   602→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   603→    )
   604→
   605→    # plan done <patterns> --attest [--note]
   606→    p_done = plan_sub.add_parser(
   607→        "done",
   608→        help="Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next step)",
   609→        epilog="""\
   610→examples:
   611→  desloppify plan done "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
   612→    --attest "I have actually removed the import and I am not gaming the score."
   613→  desloppify plan done security --note "patched XSS" \\
   614→    --attest "I have actually ..."  """,
   615→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   616→    )
   617→    p_done.add_argument(
   618→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   619→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   620→    )
   621→    p_done.add_argument(
   622→        "--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation of the fix"
   623→    )
   624→    p_done.add_argument(
   625→        "--attest",
   626→        type=str,
   627→        default=None,
   628→        help=(
   629→            "Required anti-gaming attestation. Must include BOTH keywords "
   630→            "'I have actually' and 'not gaming'."
   631→        ),
   632→    )
   633→    p_done.add_argument(
   634→        "--confirm",
   635→        action="store_true",
   636→        default=False,
   637→        help="Auto-generate attestation from --note (requires --note)",
   638→    )
   639→
   640→    # plan focus <cluster> | --clear
   641→    p_focus = plan_sub.add_parser("focus", help="Set or clear active cluster focus")
   642→    p_focus.add_argument("cluster_name", nargs="?", default=None, help="Cluster name")
   643→    p_focus.add_argument("--clear", action="store_true", help="Clear focus")
   644→
   645→    # plan cluster ...
   646→    p_cluster = plan_sub.add_parser(
   647→        "cluster",
   648→        help="Manage finding clusters",
   649→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   650→    )
   651→    cluster_sub = p_cluster.add_subparsers(dest="cluster_action")
   652→
   653→    # plan cluster create <name> [--description "..."] [--action "..."]
   654→    p_cc = cluster_sub.add_parser("create", help="Create a cluster")
   655→    p_cc.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name (slug)")
   656→    p_cc.add_argument("--description", type=str, default=None, help="Cluster description")
   657→    p_cc.add_argument("--action", type=str, default=None, help="Primary action/command for this cluster")
   658→
   659→    # plan cluster add <cluster> <patterns...>
   660→    p_ca = cluster_sub.add_parser("add", help="Add findings to a cluster")
   661→    p_ca.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   662→    p_ca.add_argument("patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN", help="Finding patterns")
   663→
   664→    # plan cluster remove <cluster> <patterns...>
   665→    p_cr = cluster_sub.add_parser("remove", help="Remove findings from a cluster")
   666→    p_cr.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   667→    p_cr.add_argument("patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN", help="Finding patterns")
   668→
   669→    # plan cluster delete <name>
   670→    p_cd = cluster_sub.add_parser("delete", help="Delete a cluster")
   671→    p_cd.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   672→
   673→    # plan cluster move <cluster> <position> [target]
   674→    p_cm = cluster_sub.add_parser("move", help="Move cluster as a block")
   675→    p_cm.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   676→    p_cm.add_argument(
   677→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
   678→        help="Where to move",
   679→    )
   680→    p_cm.add_argument("target", nargs="?", default=None, help="Target or offset")
   681→
   682→    # plan cluster show <name>
   683→    p_cs = cluster_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show cluster details and members")
   684→    p_cs.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   685→
   686→    # plan cluster list
   687→    cluster_sub.add_parser("list", help="List all clusters")
   688→
   689→    # plan cluster update <name> [--description "..."] [--steps "..." ...]
   690→    p_cu = cluster_sub.add_parser("update", help="Update cluster description and/or action steps")
   691→    p_cu.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   692→    p_cu.add_argument("--description", type=str, default=None, help="Cluster description")
   693→    p_cu.add_argument("--steps", nargs="+", metavar="STEP", default=None, help="Action steps list")
   694→
   695→    # plan synthesize ...
   696→    p_synth = plan_sub.add_parser(
   697→        "synthesize",
   698→        help="Staged synthesis workflow for review findings",
   699→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   700→    )
   701→    p_synth.add_argument(
   702→        "--stage",
   703→        type=str,
   704→        choices=["observe", "reflect", "organize"],
   705→        default=None,
   706→        help="Stage to record",
   707→    )
   708→    p_synth.add_argument(
   709→        "--report", type=str, default=None,
   710→        help="Stage report text",
   711→    )
   712→    p_synth.add_argument(
   713→        "--complete", action="store_true", default=False,
   714→        help="Mark synthesis complete",
   715→    )
   716→    p_synth.add_argument(
   717→        "--strategy", type=str, default=None,
   718→        help="Strategy summary (for --complete)",
   719→    )
   720→    p_synth.add_argument(
   721→        "--confirm-existing", action="store_true", default=False,
   722→        help="Fast-track confirmation of existing plan",
   723→    )
   724→    p_synth.add_argument(
   725→        "--note", type=str, default=None,
   726→        help="Note for --confirm-existing",
   727→    )
   728→    p_synth.add_argument(
   729→        "--dry-run", action="store_true", default=False,
   730→        help="Preview mode",
   731→    )
   732→
   733→
   734→def _add_viz_parser(sub) -> None:
   735→    p_viz = sub.add_parser("viz", help="Generate interactive HTML treemap")
   736→    p_viz.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   737→    p_viz.add_argument("--output", type=str, default=None, help="Output file path")
   738→    p_viz.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   739→
   740→
   741→def _add_dev_parser(sub) -> None:
   742→    p_dev = sub.add_parser("dev", help="Developer utilities")
   743→    dev_sub = p_dev.add_subparsers(dest="dev_action", required=True)
   744→    d_scaffold = dev_sub.add_parser(
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   105→    plan: PlanModel,
   106→    state: StateModel,
   107→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   108→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   109→
   110→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   111→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   112→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   113→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   114→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   118→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   119→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   120→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   121→
   122→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   123→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   124→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   125→        fid for fid in order
   126→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   127→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   128→        and fid not in stale_ids
   129→    ]
   130→    for fid in to_remove:
   131→        order.remove(fid)
   132→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   133→
   134→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front ----------------------------
   135→    existing = set(order)
   136→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   137→        if uid not in existing:
   138→            order.insert(0, uid)
   139→            result.injected.append(uid)
   140→
   141→    return result
   142→
   143→
   144→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   145→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   146→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→
   148→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   149→    plan: PlanModel,
   150→    state: StateModel,
   151→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   152→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   153→
   154→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   155→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   156→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   157→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   158→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   159→    """
   160→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   161→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   162→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   163→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   164→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   165→
   166→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   167→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   168→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   169→        fid for fid in order
   170→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   171→        and fid not in stale_ids
   172→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   173→    ]
   174→    for fid in to_remove:
   175→        order.remove(fid)
   176→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   179→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   180→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   181→        existing = set(order)
   182→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   183→            if sid not in existing:
   184→                order.append(sid)
   185→                result.injected.append(sid)
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   192→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193→
   194→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   195→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   196→
   197→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   198→    """
   199→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   200→    review_ids = sorted(
   201→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   202→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   203→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   204→    )
   205→    if not review_ids:
   206→        return ""
   207→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   208→
   209→
   210→@dataclass
   211→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   212→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   213→
   214→    injected: bool = False
   215→    pruned: bool = False
   216→
   217→    @property
   218→    def changes(self) -> int:
   219→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   220→
   221→
   222→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   223→    plan: PlanModel,
   224→    state: StateModel,
   225→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   226→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   227→
   228→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   229→    """
   230→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   231→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   232→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   233→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   234→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   235→
   236→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   237→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   238→
   239→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject at front
   240→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   241→        order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   242→        result.injected = True
   243→
   244→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   245→
   246→    return result
   247→
   248→
   249→__all__ = [
   250→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   251→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   252→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   253→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   254→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   255→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   256→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   257→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   258→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   259→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   260→]
   261→
```

```
    11→
    12→def print_synthesis_guardrail_banner(*, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    13→    """Print synthesis phase guardrail banner with visible load-failure warnings."""
    14→    try:
    15→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    16→        banner = synthesis_phase_banner(resolved_plan)
    17→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    18→        print(
    19→            colorize(
    20→                "  Warning: synthesis guardrail unavailable "
    21→                f"({exc.__class__.__name__}: {exc}).",
    22→                "yellow",
    23→            ),
    24→            file=sys.stderr,
    25→        )
    26→        return
    27→    if banner:
    28→        print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    29→
    30→
    31→__all__ = ["print_synthesis_guardrail_banner"]
```
