```
    21→
    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    action: str | None = getattr(args, "action", None)
    26→    plan = load_plan()
    27→    try:
    28→        create_cluster(plan, name, description, action=action)
    29→    except ValueError as ex:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    31→        return
    32→    save_plan(plan)
    33→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    34→
    35→
    36→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    37→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    38→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    42→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    46→    if not finding_ids:
    47→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    48→        return
    49→    try:
    50→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    51→    except ValueError as ex:
    52→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    53→        return
    54→    save_plan(plan)
    55→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    56→
    57→
    58→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    59→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    60→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    61→        return
    62→
    63→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    64→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    65→
    66→    plan = load_plan()
    67→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    68→    if not finding_ids:
    69→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    70→        return
    71→    try:
    72→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    73→    except ValueError as ex:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    75→        return
    76→    save_plan(plan)
    77→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    78→
    79→
    80→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    81→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    82→    plan = load_plan()
    83→    try:
    84→        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)
    85→    except ValueError as ex:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    87→        return
    88→    save_plan(plan)
    89→    print(colorize(f"  Deleted cluster {cluster_name} ({len(orphaned)} items orphaned).", "green"))
    90→
    91→
    92→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    93→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    94→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    95→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    96→
    97→    plan = load_plan()
    98→
    99→    offset: int | None = None
   100→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   101→        try:
   102→            offset = int(target)
   103→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   104→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   105→            return
   106→        target = None
   107→
   108→    try:
   109→        count = move_cluster(plan, cluster_name, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   110→    except ValueError as ex:
   111→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   112→        return
   113→    save_plan(plan)
   114→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   115→
   116→
   117→def _print_cluster_member(idx: int, fid: str, finding: dict | None) -> None:
   118→    """Print a single cluster member line with optional finding details."""
   119→    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
   120→    if not finding:
   121→        return
   122→    file = finding.get("file", "")
   123→    lines = finding.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])
   124→    line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
   125→    if file:
   126→        print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
   127→    summary = finding.get("summary", "")
   128→    if summary:
   129→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
   130→
   131→
   132→def _load_findings_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   133→    """Load findings from state, returning empty dict on failure."""
   134→    rt = command_runtime(args)
   135→    return rt.state.get("findings", {})
   136→
   137→
   138→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   139→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   140→    plan = load_plan()
   141→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   142→    if cluster is None:
   143→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    # Header
   147→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   148→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   149→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   150→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))
   151→    print(colorize(f"  Type: {auto_tag}{key_type}", "dim"))
   152→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   153→    if desc:
   154→        print(colorize(f"  Description: {desc}", "dim"))
   155→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   156→    if action:
   157→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {action}", "dim"))
   158→
   159→    # Members
   160→    finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   161→    if not finding_ids:
   162→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))
   163→    else:
   164→        findings = _load_findings_best_effort(args)
   165→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(finding_ids)}):", "dim"))
   166→        for idx, fid in enumerate(finding_ids, 1):
   167→            _print_cluster_member(idx, fid, findings.get(fid))
   168→
   169→    # Commands
   170→    print()
   171→    print(colorize("  Commands:", "dim"))
   172→    print(colorize(f'    Resolve all:  desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   173→    print(colorize(f"    Drill in:     desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   174→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   175→
   176→
   177→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   178→    plan = load_plan()
   179→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   180→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   181→    if not clusters:
   182→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   183→        return
   184→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   185→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   186→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   187→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   188→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   189→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   190→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   191→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   192→
   193→
   194→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   195→    """Update cluster description and/or action_steps."""
   196→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   197→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
   198→    steps: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "steps", None)
   199→
   200→    if description is None and steps is None:
   201→        print(colorize("  Nothing to update. Use --description and/or --steps.", "yellow"))
   202→        return
   203→
   204→    plan = load_plan()
   205→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   206→    if cluster is None:
   207→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    if description is not None:
   211→        cluster["description"] = description
   212→    if steps is not None:
   213→        cluster["action_steps"] = list(steps)
   214→    cluster["user_modified"] = True
   215→
   216→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
   217→
   218→    cluster["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   219→    save_plan(plan)
   220→    print(colorize(f"  Updated cluster: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   221→
   222→
   223→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   224→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   225→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   226→    dispatch = {
   227→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   228→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   229→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   230→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   231→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   232→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   233→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   234→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   235→    }
   236→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   237→    if handler is None:
   238→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   239→        return
   240→    handler(args)
   241→
   242→
   243→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   244→
```

```
    68→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):

    71→
    72→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    73→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    74→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
    75→        return
    76→    try:
    77→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    78→        _save_config(config)
    79→    except OSError as exc:
    80→        _ = exc
    81→        config["needs_rescan"] = True
    82→
    83→
    84→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    85→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    86→    return bool(
    87→        plan.get("queue_order")
    88→        or plan.get("overrides")
    89→        or plan.get("clusters")
    90→        or plan.get("skipped")
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    95→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    96→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    97→        return False
    98→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    99→    if recon.resurfaced:
   100→        print(
   101→            colorize(
   102→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   103→                "cyan",
   104→            )
   105→        )
   106→    return bool(recon.changes)
   107→
   108→
   109→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   110→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
   111→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   112→    if sync.injected:
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
   116→                "cyan",
   117→            )
   118→        )
   119→    return bool(sync.changes)
   120→
   121→
   122→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   123→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
   124→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   125→    if sync.pruned:
   126→        print(
   127→            colorize(
   128→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   129→                "cyan",
   130→            )
   131→        )
   132→    if sync.injected:
   133→        print(
   134→            colorize(
   135→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} stale subjective dimension(s) queued for refresh.",
   136→                "cyan",
   137→            )
   138→        )
   139→    return bool(sync.changes)
   140→

   142→def _sync_auto_clusters(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   143→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   144→    return bool(auto_cluster_findings(plan, state))
   145→
   146→
   147→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   148→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   149→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   150→        return False
   151→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   152→    if scores.strict is None:
   153→        return False
   154→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   155→        "strict": scores.strict,
   156→        "overall": scores.overall,
   157→        "objective": scores.objective,
   158→        "verified": scores.verified,
   159→    }
   160→    return True
   161→
   162→
   163→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   164→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   165→) -> bool:
   166→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   167→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   168→        return False
   169→
   170→    try:
   171→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
   172→
   173→        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
   174→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   175→        _ = exc
   176→        return False
   177→    if not queue_empty:
   178→        return False
   179→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   180→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   181→    return True

   183→
   184→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   185→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   186→    try:
   187→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   188→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   189→        dirty = False
   190→
   191→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   192→            dirty = True
   193→        if _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions):
   194→            dirty = True
   195→        if _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_stale_dimensions):
   196→            dirty = True
   197→        if _sync_auto_clusters(plan, runtime.state):
   198→            dirty = True
   199→        synth_sync = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   200→        if synth_sync.changes:
   201→            dirty = True
   202→            if synth_sync.injected:
   203→                print(
   204→                    colorize(
   205→                        "  Plan: synthesis needed — review findings changed since last synthesis.",
   206→                        "cyan",
   207→                    )
   208→                )
   209→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   210→        if seeded:
   211→            dirty = True
   212→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   213→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   214→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   215→            dirty = True
   216→
   217→        if dirty:
   218→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   219→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   220→        _ = exc
   221→
   222→
   223→def _state_subjective_assessments(
   224→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   225→) -> dict[str, object]:
   226→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
   227→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   228→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
   229→        return assessments
   230→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   231→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
   232→    )
   233→
   234→
   235→def _state_lang_capabilities(
   236→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   237→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   238→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
   239→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   240→    if capabilities is None:
   241→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   242→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   243→        return normalized
   244→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
   245→        return capabilities
   246→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   247→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"
   248→    )
   249→
   250→
   251→def _state_findings(state: state_mod.StateModel) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   252→    """Return normalized finding map from state."""
   253→    findings = state.get("findings")
   254→    if isinstance(findings, dict):
   255→        return findings
   256→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   257→        "state.findings must be an object; state file appears corrupted"
   258→    )

   261→def _subjective_reset_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   262→    """Resolve subjective dimensions that should reset on scan baseline reset."""
   263→    return resettable_default_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name)

   267→class ScanRuntime:

   269→

   272→    state: state_mod.StateModel

   308→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   309→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   310→    config: dict[str, object],
   311→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   312→    lang: LangRun | None,
   313→) -> LangRun | None:
   314→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   315→    if not lang:
   316→        return None
   317→
   318→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   319→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   320→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   321→        lang,
   322→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   323→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   324→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   325→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   326→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   327→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   328→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   329→        ),
   330→    )
   331→
   332→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   333→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   334→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   335→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   336→    }
   337→    return runtime_lang
   338→
   339→
   340→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   341→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   342→    *,
   343→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   344→) -> int:
   345→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   346→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   347→
   348→    reset_keys = {
   349→        key.strip()
   350→        for key in assessments
   351→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   352→    }
   353→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   354→
   355→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   356→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   357→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   358→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   359→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   360→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   361→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   362→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   363→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   364→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   365→            payload.pop("components", None)
   366→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   367→            continue
   368→        assessments[key] = {
   369→            "score": 0.0,
   370→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   371→            "assessed_at": now,
   372→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   373→            "placeholder": True,
   374→        }
   375→    return len(reset_keys)
   376→
   377→
   378→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   379→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   380→) -> int:
   381→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   382→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   383→
   384→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   385→    expired = 0
   386→    for payload in assessments.values():
   387→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   388→            continue
   389→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   390→            continue
   391→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   392→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   393→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   394→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   395→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   396→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   397→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   398→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   399→        payload.pop("components", None)
   400→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   401→        expired += 1
   402→    return expired
   403→
   404→
   405→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   406→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   407→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   408→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   409→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   410→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   411→    path = Path(args.path)
   412→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   413→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   414→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   415→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   416→        state
   417→    )
   418→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   419→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   420→            state,
   421→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   422→        )
   423→
   424→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   425→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   426→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   427→
   428→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   429→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   430→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   431→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   432→
   433→    return ScanRuntime(
   434→        args=args,
   435→        state_path=state_file,
   436→        state=state,
   437→        path=path,
   438→        config=config,
   439→        lang=lang,
   440→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   441→        profile=profile,
   442→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   443→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   444→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   445→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   446→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   447→    )
   448→
   449→
   450→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   451→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   452→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   453→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   454→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   455→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   456→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   457→        return findings
   458→
   459→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   460→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   461→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   462→    )
   463→    if not stale:
   464→        return findings
   465→
   466→    augmented = list(findings)
   467→    augmented.extend(stale)
   468→    for stale_finding in stale:
   469→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   470→    return augmented
   471→
   472→
   473→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   474→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   475→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   476→    *,
   477→    decay_scans: int,
   478→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   479→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   480→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   481→        findings,
   482→        state=runtime.state,
   483→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   484→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   485→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   486→    )
   487→
   488→
   489→def run_scan_generation(
   490→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   491→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   492→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   493→    enable_file_cache()
   494→    enable_parse_cache()
   495→    try:
   496→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   497→            runtime.path,
   498→            lang=runtime.lang,
   499→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   500→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   501→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   502→                profile=runtime.profile,
   503→            ),
   504→        )
   505→    finally:
   506→        disable_parse_cache()
   507→        disable_file_cache()
   508→
   509→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   510→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   511→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   512→    )
   513→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   514→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   515→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   516→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   517→    )
   518→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   519→        findings,
   520→        runtime,
   521→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   522→    )
   523→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   524→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   525→
   526→
   527→def merge_scan_results(
   528→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   529→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   530→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   531→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   532→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   533→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   534→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   535→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   536→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   537→
   538→    if not path_changed:
   539→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   540→    else:
   541→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   542→    prev_dim_scores = (
   543→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   544→    )
   545→
   546→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   547→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   548→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   549→
   550→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   551→
   552→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   553→        runtime.state,
   554→        findings,
   555→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   556→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   557→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   558→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   559→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   560→            potentials=potentials,
   561→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   562→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   563→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   564→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   565→        ),
   566→    )
   567→
   568→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   569→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   570→    )
   571→    state_mod.save_state(
   572→        runtime.state,
   573→        runtime.state_path,
   574→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   575→    )
   576→
   577→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   578→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   579→
   580→    return ScanMergeResult(
   581→        diff=diff,
   582→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   583→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   584→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   585→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   586→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   587→    )
   588→
   589→
   590→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   591→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   592→    config: dict[str, object],
   593→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   594→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   595→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   596→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   597→    )
   598→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   599→    open_findings = [
   600→        finding
   601→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   602→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   603→        ).values()
   604→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   605→    ]
   606→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   607→        open_findings,
   608→        budget=noise_budget,
   609→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   610→    )
   611→
   612→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   613→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   614→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   615→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   616→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   617→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   618→    )
   619→
   620→
   621→def persist_reminder_history(
   622→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   623→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   624→) -> None:
   625→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   626→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   627→        return
   628→
   629→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   630→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   631→    state_mod.save_state(
   632→        runtime.state,
   633→        runtime.state_path,
   634→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   635→    )
   636→
   637→
   638→__all__ = [
   639→    "ScanStateContractError",
   640→    "ScanMergeResult",
   641→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   642→    "ScanRuntime",
   643→    "merge_scan_results",
   644→    "persist_reminder_history",
   645→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   646→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   647→    "run_scan_generation",
   648→]
   649→
```

```
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )

   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")

   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)

   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:

   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),

   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}

   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'

   171→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   172→    """Build a synthetic T1 work item for ``synthesis::pending`` if it's in the queue.
   173→
   174→    Returns ``None`` when synthesis is not pending.
   175→    """

   177→
   178→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   182→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})

   185→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   186→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   187→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   188→    }
   189→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   190→    new_since = len(open_review_ids - synthesized_ids)
   191→    resolved_since = len(synthesized_ids - open_review_ids)
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   195→        "tier": 1,
   196→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   197→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   198→        "summary": (
   199→            f"Synthesize {len(open_review_ids)} review findings into a coherent plan"
   200→        ),
   201→        "detail": {
   202→            "total_review_findings": len(open_review_ids),
   203→            "new_since_last": new_since,
   204→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since,
   205→        },
   206→    }

   209→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   210→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   211→    if not dim_scores:
   212→        return {}
   213→
   214→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   215→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   216→    for entry in entries:
   217→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   218→        if not name:
   219→            continue
   220→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   221→        [REDACTED](name)
   222→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   223→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   224→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   225→            if not key:
   226→                continue
   227→            aliases.add(key)
   228→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   229→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   230→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   231→        for alias in aliases:
   232→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   233→    return scores

   235→
   236→def build_subjective_items(
   237→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   238→) -> list[dict]:
   239→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   240→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   241→    if not dim_scores:
   242→        return []
   243→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   244→
   245→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   246→    if not subjective_entries:
   247→        return []
   248→    unassessed_dims = {
   249→        str(name).strip()
   250→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   251→            dim_scores
   252→        )
   253→    }
   254→
   255→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   256→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   257→    for finding in findings.values():
   258→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   259→            continue
   260→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   261→        if not dim_key:
   262→            continue
   263→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   264→
   265→    items: list[dict] = []
   266→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   267→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   268→        if not name:
   269→            continue
   270→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   271→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   272→            continue
   273→
   274→        [REDACTED](name)
   275→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   276→        cli_keys = [
   277→            str(key).strip().lower()
   278→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   279→            if str(key).strip()
   280→        ]
   281→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   282→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   283→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   284→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   285→            name in unassessed_dims
   286→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   287→        )
   288→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   289→        if is_unassessed:
   290→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   291→        elif is_stale:
   292→            if cli_keys:
   293→                primary_command = (
   294→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   295→                )
   296→            else:
   297→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   298→        else:
   299→            if open_review > 0:
   300→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   301→            elif cli_keys:
   302→                primary_command = (
   303→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   304→                )
   305→            else:
   306→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   307→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   308→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   309→        items.append(
   310→            {
   311→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   312→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   313→                "file": ".",
   314→                "tier": 4,
   315→                "effective_tier": 4,
   316→                "confidence": "medium",
   317→                "summary": summary,
   318→                "detail": {
   319→                    "dimension_name": name,
   320→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   321→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   322→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   323→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   324→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   325→                },
   326→                "status": "open",
   327→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   328→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   329→            }
   330→        )
   331→    return items
   332→
   333→
   334→__all__ = [
   335→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   336→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   337→    "build_subjective_items",
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   100→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   101→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   102→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   103→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   104→
   105→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   106→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   107→        try:
   108→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   109→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   110→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   111→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   112→                to_prune.append(fid)
   113→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   114→            to_prune.append(fid)
   115→
   116→    for fid in to_prune:
   117→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   118→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   119→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   120→
   121→    return to_prune
   122→
   123→
   124→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> ReconcileResult:
   128→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   129→
   130→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   131→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   132→    """
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    result = ReconcileResult()
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   137→
   138→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   139→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   140→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   142→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   143→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   144→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   145→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   146→
   147→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   148→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   149→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   150→    referenced_ids = {
   151→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   152→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   153→        and fid != SYNTHESIS_ID
   154→    }
   155→
   156→    # Check each referenced ID
   157→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   158→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   159→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   160→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   161→                result.changes += 1
   162→
   163→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead findings, delete empty epics
   164→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   165→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   166→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   167→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   168→            continue
   169→        finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   170→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if _is_finding_alive(state, fid)]
   171→        if alive_ids != finding_ids:
   172→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive_ids
   173→            result.changes += 1
   174→        if not alive_ids:
   175→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   176→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   177→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   178→        result.changes += 1
   179→
   180→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   181→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   182→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   183→
   184→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   185→    if resurfaced:
   186→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   187→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   188→
   189→    # Prune old superseded entries
   190→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   191→    result.pruned = pruned
   192→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   193→
   194→    return result
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = [
   198→    "ReconcileResult",
   199→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   200→]
   201→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16→# Helpers
    17→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→
    19→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    20→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    21→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    22→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    if finding_id in order:
    24→        order.remove(finding_id)
    25→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_position(
    29→    order: list[str],
    30→    position: str,
    31→    target: str | None = None,
    32→    offset: int | None = None,
    33→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    34→) -> int:
    35→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    36→
    37→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    38→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    39→    """
    40→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    41→
    42→    if position == "top":
    43→        return 0
    44→    if position == "bottom":
    45→        return len(order)
    46→
    47→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    48→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    49→
    50→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    51→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    52→            order,
    53→            moving,
    54→            position=position,
    55→            offset=offset,
    56→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
    57→        )
    58→
    59→    return len(order)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _resolve_relative_position(
    63→    order: list[str],
    64→    moving: set[str],
    65→    *,
    66→    position: str,
    67→    target: str,
    68→) -> int:
    69→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    70→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    71→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
    72→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
    73→
    74→
    75→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
    76→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
    77→        if item_id == target:
    78→            return i
    79→    return None
    80→
    81→
    82→def _resolve_offset_position(
    83→    order: list[str],
    84→    moving: set[str],
    85→    *,
    86→    position: str,
    87→    offset: int,
    88→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
    89→) -> int:
    90→    if not finding_ids:
    91→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
    92→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
    93→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    94→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
    95→    if current_idx is None:
    96→        if position == "up":
    97→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    98→        return len(clean_order)
    99→    if position == "up":
   100→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   101→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   102→
   103→
   104→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   105→# Move items
   106→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   107→
   108→def move_items(
   109→    plan: PlanModel,
   110→    finding_ids: list[str],
   111→    position: str,
   112→    target: str | None = None,
   113→    offset: int | None = None,
   114→) -> int:
   115→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   118→
   119→    # Remove from skipped if present
   120→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   121→    for fid in finding_ids:
   122→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   123→
   124→    # Remove from current position in order
   125→    for fid in finding_ids:
   126→        if fid in order:
   127→            order.remove(fid)
   128→
   129→    # Resolve insertion point
   130→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   131→
   132→    # Insert in original order
   133→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   134→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   135→
   136→    return len(finding_ids)
   137→
   138→
   139→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   140→# Skip / unskip
   141→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   142→
   143→def skip_items(
   144→    plan: PlanModel,
   145→    finding_ids: list[str],
   146→    *,
   147→    kind: str = "temporary",
   148→    reason: str | None = None,
   149→    note: str | None = None,
   150→    attestation: str | None = None,
   151→    review_after: int | None = None,
   152→    scan_count: int = 0,
   153→) -> int:
   154→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   155→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   156→    now = utc_now()
   157→    count = 0
   158→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   159→    for fid in finding_ids:
   160→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   161→        skipped[fid] = {
   162→            "finding_id": fid,
   163→            "kind": kind,
   164→            "reason": reason,
   165→            "note": note,
   166→            "attestation": attestation,
   167→            "created_at": now,
   168→            "review_after": review_after,
   169→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   170→        }
   171→        count += 1
   172→    return count
   173→
   174→
   175→def unskip_items(
   176→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   177→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   178→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   179→
   180→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   181→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   182→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   183→    """
   184→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   185→    count = 0
   186→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   187→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   188→    for fid in finding_ids:
   189→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   190→        if entry is not None:
   191→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   192→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   193→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   194→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   195→            count += 1
   196→    return count, need_reopen
   197→
   198→
   199→def resurface_stale_skips(
   200→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   201→) -> list[str]:
   202→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   203→
   204→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   205→    """
   206→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   207→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   208→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   209→    for fid in list(skipped):
   210→        entry = skipped[fid]
   211→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   212→            continue
   213→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   214→        if review_after is None:
   215→            continue
   216→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   217→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   218→            skipped.pop(fid)
   219→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   220→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   221→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   222→    return resurfaced
   223→
   224→
   225→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   226→# Describe / annotate
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→
   229→def describe_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→def annotate_finding(
   243→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   244→) -> None:
   245→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   246→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   247→    now = utc_now()
   248→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   249→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   250→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   251→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   252→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   253→
   254→
   255→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→# Clusters
   257→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   258→
   259→def create_cluster(
   260→    plan: PlanModel,
   261→    name: str,
   262→    description: str | None = None,
   263→    action: str | None = None,
   264→) -> Cluster:
   265→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   266→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   267→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   268→        raise ValueError(
   269→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   270→        )
   271→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   272→        raise ValueError(
   273→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for synthesis epics: {name!r}"
   274→        )
   275→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   276→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   277→    now = utc_now()
   278→    cluster: Cluster = {
   279→        "name": name,
   280→        "description": description,
   281→        "finding_ids": [],
   282→        "created_at": now,
   283→        "updated_at": now,
   284→        "auto": False,
   285→        "cluster_key": "",
   286→        "action": action,
   287→        "user_modified": False,
   288→    }
   289→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   290→    return cluster
   291→
   292→
   293→def add_to_cluster(
   294→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   295→) -> int:
   296→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   297→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   298→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   299→    if cluster is None:
   300→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   301→
   302→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   303→    count = 0
   304→    now = utc_now()
   305→    for fid in finding_ids:
   306→        if fid not in member_ids:
   307→            member_ids.append(fid)
   308→            count += 1
   309→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   310→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   311→        if fid not in overrides:
   312→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   313→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   314→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   315→
   316→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   317→    return count
   318→
   319→
   320→def remove_from_cluster(
   321→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   322→) -> int:
   323→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   324→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   325→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   326→    if cluster is None:
   327→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   328→
   329→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   330→    now = utc_now()
   331→    count = 0
   332→    for fid in finding_ids:
   333→        if fid in member_ids:
   334→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   335→            count += 1
   336→        # Clear cluster from override
   337→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   338→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   339→            override["cluster"] = None
   340→            override["updated_at"] = now
   341→
   342→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   343→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   344→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   345→
   346→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   347→    return count
   348→
   349→
   350→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   351→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   352→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   353→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   354→    if cluster is None:
   355→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   356→
   357→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   358→    now = utc_now()
   359→    for fid in orphaned:
   360→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   361→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   362→            override["cluster"] = None
   363→            override["updated_at"] = now
   364→
   365→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   366→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   367→
   368→    return orphaned
   369→
   370→
   371→def move_cluster(
   372→    plan: PlanModel,
   373→    cluster_name: str,
   374→    position: str,
   375→    target: str | None = None,
   376→    offset: int | None = None,
   377→) -> int:
   378→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   379→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   380→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   381→    if cluster is None:
   382→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   383→
   384→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   385→    if not member_ids:
   386→        return 0
   387→
   388→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   389→
   390→
   391→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   392→# Focus
   393→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   394→
   395→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   396→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   397→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   398→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   399→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   400→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   401→
   402→
   403→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   404→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   405→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   406→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   407→
   408→
   409→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   410→# Reset
   411→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   412→
   413→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   414→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   415→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   416→    plan.clear()
   417→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   418→        plan[k] = v
   419→    plan["created"] = created
   420→
   421→
   422→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   423→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   424→
   425→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   426→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   427→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   428→    """
   429→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   430→    found = 0
   431→
   432→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   433→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   434→    for fid in finding_ids:
   435→        was_present = False
   436→        if fid in order:
   437→            order.remove(fid)
   438→            was_present = True
   439→        if fid in skipped:
   440→            skipped.pop(fid)
   441→            was_present = True
   442→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   443→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   444→            if fid in ids:
   445→                ids.remove(fid)
   446→                was_present = True
   447→        if was_present:
   448→            found += 1
   449→
   450→    return found
   451→
   452→
   453→__all__ = [
   454→    "add_to_cluster",
   455→    "annotate_finding",
   456→    "clear_focus",
   457→    "create_cluster",
   458→    "delete_cluster",
   459→    "describe_finding",
   460→    "move_cluster",
   461→    "move_items",
   462→    "purge_ids",
   463→    "remove_from_cluster",
   464→    "reset_plan",
   465→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   466→    "set_focus",
   467→    "skip_items",
   468→    "unskip_items",
   469→]
   470→
```

```
1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (

    13→
    14→
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    28→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = None
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    include_subjective: bool = True
    33→    no_tier_fallback: bool = True
    34→
    35→
    36→def build_open_plan_queue(
    37→    state: dict[str, Any],
    38→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    39→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    40→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    41→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    42→    resolved_scan_path: str | None
    43→    if isinstance(policy.scan_path, str):
    44→        resolved_scan_path = policy.scan_path
    45→    else:
    46→        raw_state_path = state.get("scan_path")
    47→        resolved_scan_path = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, str) else None
    48→    return build_work_queue(
    49→        state,
    50→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    51→            tier=policy.tier,
    52→            count=policy.count,
    53→            scan_path=resolved_scan_path,
    54→            status="open",
    55→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    56→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    57→            no_tier_fallback=policy.no_tier_fallback,
    58→        ),
    59→    )
    60→
    61→
    62→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]
    63→
```

```
1→"""Stable review import mode normalization for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Literal
     6→
     7→ReviewImportMode = Literal[
     8→    "auto",
     9→    "trusted_internal",
    10→    "attested_external",
    11→    "manual_override",
    12→]
    13→
    14→_ALLOWED_MODES: set[str] = {
    15→    "auto",
    16→    "trusted_internal",
    17→    "attested_external",
    18→    "manual_override",
    19→}
    20→
    21→
    22→def normalize_review_import_mode(raw: str | None) -> ReviewImportMode:
    23→    """Normalize user/caller import mode text to one stable mode token."""
    24→    mode = (raw or "auto").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    25→    if mode not in _ALLOWED_MODES:
    26→        allowed = ", ".join(sorted(_ALLOWED_MODES))
    27→        raise ValueError(f"invalid review import mode '{raw}'; expected one of: {allowed}")
    28→    return mode  # type: ignore[return-value]
    29→
    30→
    31→def _validate_mode_compatibility(
    32→    *,
    33→    mode: ReviewImportMode,
    34→    attested: bool,
    35→    manual: bool,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    if mode == "trusted_internal" and (attested or manual):
    38→        raise ValueError(
    39→            "import_mode=trusted_internal cannot be combined with attested/manual flags"
    40→        )
    41→    if mode == "attested_external" and manual:
    42→        raise ValueError(
    43→            "import_mode=attested_external cannot be combined with manual override flags"
    44→        )
    45→    if mode == "manual_override" and attested:
    46→        raise ValueError(
    47→            "import_mode=manual_override cannot be combined with attested external flags"
    48→        )
    49→
    50→
    51→def _apply_trusted_internal_defaults(
    52→    trusted_source: bool,
    53→    trusted_label: str | None,
    54→) -> tuple[bool, str, bool, bool]:
    55→    if not trusted_source:
    56→        trusted_source = True
    57→    if not isinstance(trusted_label, str) or not trusted_label.strip():
    58→        trusted_label = "trusted internal run-batches import"
    59→    return trusted_source, trusted_label, False, False
    60→
    61→
    62→def apply_review_import_mode(
    63→    *,
    64→    import_mode: str | None,
    65→    trusted_assessment_source: bool,
    66→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None,
    67→    attested_external: bool,
    68→    manual_override: bool,
    69→) -> tuple[bool, str | None, bool, bool]:
    70→    """Apply a canonical import mode over legacy booleans without dropping compatibility."""
    71→    mode = normalize_review_import_mode(import_mode)
    72→    trusted_source = bool(trusted_assessment_source)
    73→    trusted_label = trusted_assessment_label
    74→    attested = bool(attested_external)
    75→    manual = bool(manual_override)
    76→
    77→    _validate_mode_compatibility(mode=mode, attested=attested, manual=manual)
    78→
    79→    if mode == "trusted_internal":
    80→        return _apply_trusted_internal_defaults(trusted_source, trusted_label)
    81→    if mode == "attested_external":
    82→        return trusted_source, trusted_label, True, False
    83→    if mode == "manual_override":
    84→        return trusted_source, trusted_label, False, True
    85→
    86→    return trusted_source, trusted_label, attested, manual
    87→
    88→
    89→__all__ = [
    90→    "ReviewImportMode",
    91→    "apply_review_import_mode",
    92→    "normalize_review_import_mode",
    93→]
    94→
```

```
    45→    45→    45→def _short_id(fid: str) -> str:
    46→    46→    46→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display.
    47→    47→    47→
    48→    48→    48→    Finding IDs look like ``review::.::holistic::dim::identifier::abcdef12``.
    49→    49→    49→    Commands accept the hash suffix as a shorthand for the full ID.
    50→    50→    50→    """
    51→    51→    51→    if "::" in fid:
    52→    52→    52→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    53→    53→    53→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    54→    54→    54→            return suffix[:8]
    55→    55→    55→    return fid
    56→    56→    56→
    57→    57→    57→
    58→    58→    58→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    59→    59→    59→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for synthesis progress."""
    60→    60→    60→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    61→    61→    61→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    62→    62→    62→    for c in clusters.values():
    63→    63→    63→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    64→    64→    64→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    65→    65→    65→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    66→    66→    66→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters

    69→    69→    69→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    70→    70→    70→    """Return clusters with findings that are missing required enrichment.
    71→    71→    71→
    72→    72→    72→    Returns list of (cluster_name, list_of_missing_fields).
    73→    73→    73→    Only checks non-auto clusters (manual clusters the agent creates).

    77→    77→    77→        if not cluster.get("finding_ids"):

    81→    81→    81→        missing: list[str] = []

    83→    83→    83→            missing.append("description")
    84→    84→    84→        if not cluster.get("action_steps"):
    85→    85→    85→            missing.append("action_steps")
    86→    86→    86→        if missing:
    87→    87→    87→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    88→    88→    88→    return gaps

   177→   177→   177→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:

   193→   193→   193→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"

   196→   196→   196→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   197→   197→   197→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   198→   198→   198→
   199→   199→   199→    save_plan(plan)
   200→   200→   200→
   201→   201→   201→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
   202→   202→   202→    print(colorize(f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   203→   203→   203→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   204→   204→   204→    if effective_strategy:
```
