   434→   434→   434→def _cmd_stage_organize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   435→   435→   435→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment.
   436→   436→   436→
   437→   437→   437→    Instead of gating on a text report, validates that the plan data
   438→   438→   438→    itself has been enriched: each manual cluster needs description +
   439→   439→   439→    action_steps. This forces the agent to actually think about each
   440→   440→   440→    cluster's execution plan.
   441→   441→   441→    """
   442→   442→   442→    plan = load_plan()
   443→   443→   443→
   444→   444→   444→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   445→   445→   445→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue \u2014 nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   446→   446→   446→        return
   447→   447→   447→
   448→   448→   448→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   449→   449→   449→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   450→   450→   450→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   451→   451→   451→        if "observe" not in stages:
   452→   452→   452→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   453→   453→   453→            print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   454→   454→   454→        else:
   455→   455→   455→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   456→   456→   456→            print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   457→   457→   457→        return
   458→   458→   458→
   459→   459→   459→    # Validate: at least 1 manual cluster with findings
   460→   460→   460→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   461→   461→   461→    if not manual_clusters:
   462→   462→   462→        # Check if there are ANY clusters with findings (including auto)
   463→   463→   463→        any_clusters = [
   464→   464→   464→            name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   465→   465→   465→            if c.get("finding_ids")
   466→   466→   466→        ]
   467→   467→   467→        if any_clusters:
   468→   468→   468→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
   469→   469→   469→            print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group findings by root cause:", "dim"))
   470→   470→   470→        else:
   471→   471→   471→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with findings exist.", "red"))
   472→   472→   472→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   473→   473→   473→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <finding-patterns>", "dim"))
   474→   474→   474→        return
   475→   475→   475→
   476→   476→   476→    # Validate: all manual clusters are enriched
   477→   477→   477→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   478→   478→   478→    if gaps:
   479→   479→   479→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
   480→   480→   480→        for name, missing in gaps:
   481→   481→   481→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   482→   482→   482→        print()
   483→   483→   483→        print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
   484→   484→   484→        print(colorize(
   485→   485→   485→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" '
   486→   486→   486→            '--steps "step 1" "step 2"',
   487→   487→   487→            "dim",
   488→   488→   488→        ))
   489→   489→   489→        return
   490→   490→   490→
   491→   491→   491→    # Require report — the agent must summarize what they organized and why
   492→   492→   492→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   493→   493→   493→    if not report:
   494→   494→   494→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   495→   495→   495→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   496→   496→   496→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory findings before clustering?", "dim"))
   497→   497→   497→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   498→   498→   498→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   499→   499→   499→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   500→   500→   500→        return
   501→   501→   501→    if len(report) < 100:
   502→   502→   502→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   503→   503→   503→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   504→   504→   504→        return
   505→   505→   505→
   506→   506→   506→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   507→   507→   507→    stages["organize"] = {
   508→   508→   508→        "stage": "organize",
   509→   509→   509→        "report": report,
   510→   510→   510→        "cited_ids": [],
   511→   511→   511→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   512→   512→   512→        "finding_count": len(manual_clusters),
   513→   513→   513→    }
   514→   514→   514→    save_plan(plan)
   515→   515→   515→
   516→   516→   516→    print(colorize(
   517→   517→   517→        f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).",
   518→   518→   518→        "green",
   519→   519→   519→    ))
   520→   520→   520→    # Show cluster summary
   521→   521→   521→    for name in manual_clusters:
   522→   522→   522→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   523→   523→   523→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   524→   524→   524→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   525→   525→   525→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   526→   526→   526→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {len(cluster.get('finding_ids', []))} findings, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}", "dim"))
   527→   527→   527→
   528→   528→   528→    # Print the organize report prominently — this is the debrief
   529→   529→   529→    print()
   530→   530→   530→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Prioritized organization (share with user) ───────────┐", "cyan"))
   531→   531→   531→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   532→   532→   532→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   533→   533→   533→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   534→   534→   534→    print()
   535→   535→   535→    print(colorize(
   536→   536→   536→        "  IMPORTANT: Present your prioritized organization to the user. Explain",
   537→   537→   537→        "yellow",
   538→   538→   538→    ))
   539→   539→   539→    print(colorize(
   540→   540→   540→        "  each cluster, why it exists, and your explicit priority ordering —",
   541→   541→   541→        "yellow",
   542→   542→   542→    ))
   543→   543→   543→    print(colorize(
   544→   544→   544→        "  which cluster comes first, second, third, what depends on what,",
   545→   545→   545→        "yellow",
   546→   546→   546→    ))
   547→   547→   547→    print(colorize(
   548→   548→   548→        "  and why that ordering matters.",
   549→   549→   549→        "yellow",
   550→   550→   550→    ))
   551→   551→   551→    print()
   552→   552→   552→    print(colorize(
   553→   553→   553→        '  Next: desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "plan summary..."',
   554→   554→   554→        "dim",
   555→   555→   555→    ))

   562→   562→   562→def _cmd_synthesize_complete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   563→   563→   563→    """Complete synthesis \u2014 requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
   564→   564→   564→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   565→   565→   565→    plan = load_plan()
   566→   566→   566→
   567→   567→   567→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   568→   568→   568→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue \u2014 nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
   569→   569→   569→        return
   570→   570→   570→
   571→   571→   571→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   572→   572→   572→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   573→   573→   573→
   574→   574→   574→    # Require organize stage (or confirmed-existing)
   575→   575→   575→    if "organize" not in stages:
   576→   576→   576→        if "observe" not in stages:
   577→   577→   577→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   578→   578→   578→            print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   579→   579→   579→        else:
   580→   580→   580→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   581→   581→   581→            gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   582→   582→   582→            if gaps:
   583→   583→   583→                print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   584→   584→   584→                for name, missing in gaps:
   585→   585→   585→                    print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   586→   586→   586→                print(colorize(
   587→   587→   587→                    '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"',
   588→   588→   588→                    "dim",
   589→   589→   589→                ))
   590→   590→   590→                print(colorize(
   591→   591→   591→                    f"  Then: {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}",
   592→   592→   592→                    "dim",
   593→   593→   593→                ))
   594→   594→   594→            else:
   595→   595→   595→                manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   596→   596→   596→                if manual:
   597→   597→   597→                    print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   598→   598→   598→                    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   599→   599→   599→                else:

   712→   712→   712→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   713→   713→   713→        print(colorize("  You must compare against completed work first.", "dim"))
   714→   714→   714→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   715→   715→   715→        return
   716→   716→   716→
   717→   717→   717→    # Require a prior completed synthesis — can't skip the full flow on first run
   718→   718→   718→    prior_strategy = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   719→   719→   719→    if not prior_strategy:
   720→   720→   720→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no prior synthesis has been completed.", "red"))
   721→   721→   721→        print(colorize("  The full OBSERVE \u2192 REFLECT \u2192 ORGANIZE \u2192 COMMIT flow is required the first time.", "dim"))
   722→   722→   722→        print(colorize(f"  Create and enrich clusters, then: {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   723→   723→   723→        return
   724→   724→   724→
   725→   725→   725→    # Require existing enriched clusters
   726→   726→   726→    clusters_with_findings = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   727→   727→   727→    if not clusters_with_findings:
   728→   728→   728→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no clusters with findings exist.", "red"))
   729→   729→   729→        print(colorize("  Use the full organize flow instead.", "dim"))
   730→   730→   730→        return
   731→   731→   731→
   732→   732→   732→    # Require note
   733→   733→   733→    if not note:
   734→   734→   734→        print(colorize("  --note is required for confirm-existing.", "red"))
   735→   735→   735→        print(colorize('  Explain why the existing plan is still valid (min 100 chars).', "dim"))
   736→   736→   736→        return
   737→   737→   737→    if len(note) < 100:
   738→   738→   738→        print(colorize(f"  Note too short: {len(note)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   739→   739→   739→        return
   740→   740→   740→
   741→   741→   741→    # Require strategy
   742→   742→   742→    if not strategy:
   743→   743→   743→        print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   744→   744→   744→        existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   745→   745→   745→        if existing:
   746→   746→   746→            print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   747→   747→   747→        return
   748→   748→   748→
   749→   749→   749→    # Strategy length check (unless "same")
   750→   750→   750→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" and len(strategy.strip()) < 200:
   751→   751→   751→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   752→   752→   752→        return
   753→   753→   753→
   754→   754→   754→    # Validate: note cites at least 1 new/changed finding (if there are any)
   755→   755→   755→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   756→   756→   756→    state = runtime.state
   757→   757→   757→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   758→   758→   758→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   759→   759→   759→    if new_ids:
   760→   760→   760→        valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   761→   761→   761→        cited = extract_finding_citations(note, valid_ids)
   762→   762→   762→        new_cited = cited & new_ids
   763→   763→   763→        if not new_cited:
   764→   764→   764→            print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed finding.", "red"))
   765→   765→   765→            print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new finding(s) since last synthesis:", "dim"))
   766→   766→   766→            for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   767→   767→   767→                print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   768→   768→   768→            if len(new_ids) > 5:
   769→   769→   769→                print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   770→   770→   770→            return
   771→   771→   771→
   772→   772→   772→    # Record organize as confirmed-existing and complete
   773→   773→   773→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   774→   774→   774→    stages["organize"] = {
   775→   775→   775→        "stage": "organize",
   776→   776→   776→        "report": f"[confirmed-existing] {note}",
   777→   777→   777→        "cited_ids": [],
   778→   778→   778→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   779→   779→   779→        "finding_count": len(clusters_with_findings),
   780→   780→   780→    }
   781→   781→   781→
   782→   782→   782→    _apply_completion(args, plan, strategy)
   783→   783→   783→    print(colorize("  Confirmed existing plan \u2014 synthesis complete.", "green"))
   784→   784→   784→
   785→   785→   785→
   786→   786→   786→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   787→   787→   787→# Reflect dashboard
   788→   788→   788→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   789→   789→   789→
   790→   790→   790→def _print_reflect_dashboard(si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   791→   791→   791→    """Show completed clusters, resolved findings, and recurring patterns."""
   792→   792→   792→    # si is a SynthesisInput
   793→   793→   793→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   794→   794→   794→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_findings", {})
   795→   795→   795→    open_findings = getattr(si, "open_findings", {})
   796→   796→   796→
   797→   797→   797→    if completed:
   798→   798→   798→        print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   799→   799→   799→        for c in completed[:10]:
   800→   800→   800→            name = c.get("name", "?")
   801→   801→   801→            count = len(c.get("finding_ids", []))
   802→   802→   802→            thesis = c.get("thesis", "")
   803→   803→   803→            print(f"    {name}: {count} findings")
   804→   804→   804→            if thesis:
   805→   805→   805→                print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   806→   806→   806→            for step in c.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   807→   807→   807→                print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   808→   808→   808→        if len(completed) > 10:
   809→   809→   809→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   810→   810→   810→
   811→   811→   811→    if resolved:
   812→   812→   812→        print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved findings since last synthesis: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   813→   813→   813→        for fid, f in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   814→   814→   814→            status = f.get("status", "")
   815→   815→   815→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   816→   816→   816→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   817→   817→   817→            dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   818→   818→   818→            print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   819→   819→   819→            print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   820→   820→   820→        if len(resolved) > 10:
   821→   821→   821→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   822→   822→   822→
   823→   823→   823→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(open_findings, resolved)
   824→   824→   824→    if recurring:
   825→   825→   825→        print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   826→   826→   826→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   827→   827→   827→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   828→   828→   828→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   829→   829→   829→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   830→   830→   830→            print(colorize(
   831→   831→   831→                f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}",
   832→   832→   832→                "yellow",
   833→   833→   833→            ))
   834→   834→   834→    elif not completed and not resolved:
   835→   835→   835→        print(colorize("\n  First synthesis — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   836→   836→   836→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   837→   837→   837→        print(colorize("  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?", "dim"))
   838→   838→   838→        print(colorize("  - Which findings will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   839→   839→   839→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
   840→   840→   840→
   841→   841→   841→
   842→   842→   842→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   843→   843→   843→# Dashboard (default view)
   844→   844→   844→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   845→   845→   845→
   846→   846→   846→def _cmd_synthesize_dashboard(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   847→   847→   847→    """Default view: show findings, stage progress, and next command."""
   848→   848→   848→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   849→   849→   849→    state = runtime.state
   850→   850→   850→    plan = load_plan()
   851→   851→   851→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   852→   852→   852→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   853→   853→   853→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   854→   854→   854→
   855→   855→   855→    print(colorize("  Epic synthesis \u2014 manual", "bold"))
   856→   856→   856→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   857→   857→   857→    print(f"  Open review findings: {len(si.open_findings)}")
   858→   858→   858→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
   859→   859→   859→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
   860→   860→   860→    if si.existing_epics:
   861→   861→   861→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
   862→   862→   862→    if si.new_since_last:
   863→   863→   863→        print(f"  New since last synthesis: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
   864→   864→   864→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   865→   865→   865→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   866→   866→   866→
   867→   867→   867→    # Stage progress (with enrichment gaps)
   868→   868→   868→    print()
   869→   869→   869→    _print_stage_progress(stages, plan)
   870→   870→   870→    if meta.get("stage_refresh_required"):
   871→   871→   871→        print(
   872→   872→   872→            colorize(
   873→   873→   873→                "  Note: review findings changed since stage progress started; "
   874→   874→   874→                "refresh stage reports before completion.",
   875→   875→   875→                "yellow",
   876→   876→   876→            )
   877→   877→   877→        )
   878→   878→   878→
   879→   879→   879→    # --- Action guidance (shown early so agents see what to do first) ---
   880→   880→   880→    print()
   881→   881→   881→    if "observe" not in stages:
   882→   882→   882→        print(colorize("  Next step:", "yellow"))
   883→   883→   883→        print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   884→   884→   884→        print(colorize("    (themes, root causes, contradictions between findings — NOT a list of IDs)", "dim"))
   885→   885→   885→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   886→   886→   886→        print(colorize("  Next step: use the completed work and patterns below to write your reflect report.", "yellow"))
   887→   887→   887→        print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_REFLECT}")
   888→   888→   888→        print(colorize("    (Contradictions, recurring patterns, which direction to take, what to defer)", "dim"))
   889→   889→   889→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   890→   890→   890→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   891→   891→   891→        manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   892→   892→   892→
   893→   893→   893→        if not manual:
   894→   894→   894→            print(colorize("  Next steps:", "yellow"))
   895→   895→   895→            print("    0. Defer contradictory findings: `desloppify plan skip <hash>`")
   896→   896→   896→            print(f"    1. Create clusters:  {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   897→   897→   897→            print(f"    2. Add findings:     {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   898→   898→   898→            print(f"    3. Enrich clusters:  {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")
   899→   899→   899→            print(f"    4. Record stage:     {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   900→   900→   900→        elif gaps:
   901→   901→   901→            print(colorize("  Enrich these clusters before recording organize:", "yellow"))
   902→   902→   902→            for name, missing in gaps:
   903→   903→   903→                print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   904→   904→   904→            print(colorize(
   905→   905→   905→                f"    Fix: {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
   906→   906→   906→                "dim",
   907→   907→   907→            ))
   908→   908→   908→            print(colorize(f"    Then: {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   909→   909→   909→        else:
   910→   910→   910→            print(colorize("  All clusters enriched! Record the organize stage:", "green"))
   911→   911→   911→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   912→   912→   912→
   913→   913→   913→        if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   914→   914→   914→            print()
   915→   915→   915→            print(colorize("  Or fast-track (if existing plan is still valid):", "dim"))
   916→   916→   916→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   917→   917→   917→    else:
   918→   918→   918→        print(colorize("  Ready to complete:", "green"))
   919→   919→   919→        print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE}")
   920→   920→   920→        print(colorize('    (use --strategy "same" to keep existing strategy)', "dim"))
   921→   921→   921→
   922→   922→   922→    # --- Prior stage reports (context for current action) ---
   923→   923→   923→    if "observe" in stages:
   924→   924→   924→        obs_report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   925→   925→   925→        if obs_report:
   926→   926→   926→            print(colorize("\n  Your observe analysis:", "dim"))
   927→   927→   927→            for line in obs_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   928→   928→   928→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   929→   929→   929→            if len(obs_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   930→   930→   930→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   931→   931→   931→    if "reflect" in stages:
   932→   932→   932→        ref_report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   933→   933→   933→        if ref_report:
   934→   934→   934→            print(colorize("\n  Your reflect strategy:", "dim"))
   935→   935→   935→            for line in ref_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   936→   936→   936→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   937→   937→   937→            if len(ref_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   938→   938→   938→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   939→   939→   939→
   940→   940→   940→    # --- Findings data ---
   941→   941→   941→    # Group findings by dimension with suggestions to surface contradictions
   942→   942→   942→    by_dim: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
   943→   943→   943→    for fid, f in si.open_findings.items():
   944→   944→   944→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   945→   945→   945→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   946→   946→   946→        by_dim[dim].append((fid, f))
   947→   947→   947→
   948→   948→   948→    print(colorize("\n  Review findings by dimension:", "cyan"))
   949→   949→   949→    print(colorize("  (Look for contradictions: findings in the same dimension that", "dim"))
   950→   950→   950→    print(colorize("  recommend opposite changes. These must be resolved before clustering.)", "dim"))
   951→   951→   951→    max_per_dim = 5
   952→   952→   952→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-len(by_dim[d]), d)):
   953→   953→   953→        items = by_dim[dim]
   954→   954→   954→        print(colorize(f"\n    {dim} ({len(items)}):", "bold"))
   955→   955→   955→        for fid, f in items[:max_per_dim]:
   956→   956→   956→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   957→   957→   957→            short = _short_id(fid)
   958→   958→   958→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   959→   959→   959→            suggestion = (detail.get("suggestion") or "")[:120]
   960→   960→   960→            print(f"      [{short}] {summary}")
   961→   961→   961→            if suggestion:
   962→   962→   962→                print(colorize(f"        \u2192 {suggestion}", "dim"))
   963→   963→   963→        if len(items) > max_per_dim:
   964→   964→   964→            print(colorize(f"      ... and {len(items) - max_per_dim} more", "dim"))
   965→   965→   965→    print(colorize("\n  Use hash in commands: desloppify plan skip <hash>  |  desloppify show <hash>", "dim"))
   966→   966→   966→
   967→   967→   967→    # Show reflect dashboard when observe done, reflect not done
   968→   968→   968→    if "observe" in stages and "reflect" not in stages:
   969→   969→   969→        _print_reflect_dashboard(si, plan)
   970→   970→   970→
   971→   971→   971→    # Show current cluster progress
   972→   972→   972→    _print_progress(plan, si.open_findings)
   973→   973→   973→
   974→   974→   974→
   975→   975→   975→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   976→   976→   976→# Router
   977→   977→   977→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   978→   978→   978→
   979→   979→   979→def cmd_plan_synthesize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   980→   980→   980→    """Run epic synthesis: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT."""
   981→   981→   981→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   982→   982→   982→    state = runtime.state
   983→   983→   983→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   984→   984→   984→        return
   985→   985→   985→
   986→   986→   986→    # Route: --complete
   987→   987→   987→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
   988→   988→   988→        _cmd_synthesize_complete(args)
   989→   989→   989→        return
   990→   990→   990→
   991→   991→   991→    # Route: --confirm-existing
   992→   992→   992→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   993→   993→   993→        _cmd_confirm_existing(args)
   994→   994→   994→        return
   995→   995→   995→
   996→   996→   996→    # Route: --stage observe/reflect/organize
   997→   997→   997→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   998→   998→   998→    if stage == "observe":
   999→   999→   999→        _cmd_stage_observe(args)
  1000→  1000→  1000→        return
  1001→  1001→  1001→    if stage == "reflect":
  1002→  1002→  1002→        _cmd_stage_reflect(args)
  1003→  1003→  1003→        return
  1004→  1004→  1004→    if stage == "organize":
  1005→  1005→  1005→        _cmd_stage_organize(args)
  1006→  1006→  1006→        return
  1007→  1007→  1007→
  1008→  1008→  1008→    # Dry-run mode
  1009→  1009→  1009→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
  1010→  1010→  1010→        plan = load_plan()
  1011→  1011→  1011→        si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
  1012→  1012→  1012→        prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
  1013→  1013→  1013→        print(colorize("  Epic synthesis \u2014 dry run", "bold"))
  1014→  1014→  1014→        print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
  1015→  1015→  1015→        print(f"  Open review findings: {len(si.open_findings)}")
  1016→  1016→  1016→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
  1017→  1017→  1017→        print(f"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
  1018→  1018→  1018→        print(f"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
  1019→  1019→  1019→        print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
  1020→  1020→  1020→        print()
  1021→  1021→  1021→        print(prompt)
  1022→  1022→  1022→        return
  1023→  1023→  1023→
  1024→  1024→  1024→    # Default: dashboard
  1025→  1025→  1025→    _cmd_synthesize_dashboard(args)
  1026→  1026→  1026→
  1027→  1027→  1027→
  1028→  1028→  1028→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_synthesize"]
  1029→  1029→  1029→

     2→

     5→from dataclasses import dataclass

    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class QueueBuildOptions:
    28→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    29→
    30→    tier: int | None = None
    31→    count: int | None = 1
    32→    scan_path: str | None = None
    33→    scope: str | None = None

    46→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    47→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    48→
    49→    items: list[dict]
    50→    total: int
    51→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    52→    requested_tier: int | None
    53→    selected_tier: int | None
    54→    fallback_reason: str | None
    55→    available_tiers: list[int]
    56→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    61→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    65→    items: list[dict],
    66→    *,
    67→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    68→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    69→) -> list[dict]:
    70→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    71→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    72→        return items
    73→
    74→    subjective_items = deque(
    75→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    76→    )
    77→    objective_items = deque(
    78→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    79→    )
    80→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    81→        return items
    82→
    83→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    84→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    85→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    86→            if not objective_items:
    87→                break
    88→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    89→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    90→            if not subjective_items:
    91→                break
    92→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    93→
    94→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    95→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    96→    return interleaved
    97→
    98→
    99→def _apply_plan_order(
   100→    items: list[dict],
   101→    plan: dict,
   102→    *,
   103→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   104→    cluster: str | None = None,
   105→) -> list[dict]:
   106→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   107→
   108→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   109→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   110→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   111→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   112→    """
   113→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   114→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   115→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   116→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   117→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   118→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   119→
   120→    # Build lookup
   121→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   122→    for item in items:
   123→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   124→
   125→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   126→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   127→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   128→        if override.get("description"):
   129→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   130→        if override.get("note"):
   131→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   132→        if override.get("cluster"):
   133→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   134→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   135→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   136→                "name": cluster_name,
   137→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   138→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   139→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   140→            }
   141→
   142→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   143→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   144→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   145→    for fid in queue_order:
   146→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   147→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   148→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   149→
   150→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   151→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   152→    for item in items:
   153→        item_id = item["id"]
   154→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   155→            continue
   156→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   157→            skipped_items.append(item)
   158→        else:
   159→            remaining.append(item)
   160→
   161→    # Assign queue positions
   162→    result = ordered + remaining
   163→    if include_skipped:
   164→        result = result + skipped_items
   165→
   166→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   167→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   168→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   169→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   170→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   171→            if skip_entry:
   172→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   173→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   174→                if skip_reason:
   175→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   176→
   177→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   178→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   179→    if effective_cluster:
   180→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   181→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   182→        if cluster_member_ids:
   183→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   184→
   185→    return result
   186→
   187→
   188→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   189→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   190→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   191→        return any(
   192→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   193→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   194→        )
   195→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   196→
   197→
   198→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   199→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   200→    try:
   201→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   202→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   203→        if meta:
   204→            return meta.action_type
   205→    except ImportError as exc:
   206→        _ = exc
   207→    return "manual_fix"
   208→
   209→
   210→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   211→
   212→
   213→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   214→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   215→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   216→    if not clusters:
   217→        return items
   218→
   219→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   220→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   221→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   222→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   223→            continue
   224→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   225→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   226→
   227→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   228→        return items
   229→
   230→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   231→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   232→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   233→
   234→    for item in items:
   235→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   236→        if cname:
   237→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   238→        else:
   239→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   240→
   241→    # Build cluster meta-items
   242→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   243→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   244→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   245→        if len(members) < 2:
   246→            result.extend(members)
   247→            continue
   248→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   249→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   250→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   251→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   252→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   253→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   254→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   255→            action_type = "reorganize"
   256→        else:
   257→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   258→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   259→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   260→                action_type = "refactor"
   261→
   262→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   263→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   264→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   265→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   266→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   267→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   268→        else:
   269→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   270→
   271→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   272→        if not primary_command:
   273→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   274→
   275→        meta_item: dict = {
   276→            "id": cname,
   277→            "kind": "cluster",
   278→            "action_type": action_type,
   279→            "summary": summary,
   280→            "members": members,
   281→            "member_count": len(members),
   282→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   283→            "cluster_name": cname,
   284→            "cluster_auto": True,
   285→            "confidence": "high",
   286→            "detector": detector,
   287→            "file": "",
   288→        }
   289→        result.append(meta_item)
   290→
   291→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   292→    return result
   293→
   294→
   295→def build_work_queue(
   296→    state: StateModel,
   297→    *,
   298→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   299→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   300→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   301→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   302→
   303→    status = resolved_options.status
   304→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   305→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   306→    try:
   307→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   308→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   309→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   310→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   311→
   312→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   313→        state,
   314→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   315→        status_filter=status,
   316→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   317→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   318→    )
   319→
   320→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   321→    if (
   322→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   323→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   324→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   325→    ):
   326→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   327→            state,
   328→            state.get("findings", {}),
   329→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   330→        )
   331→        for item in subjective_items:
   332→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   333→                all_items.append(item)
   334→
   335→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   336→
   337→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   338→    if resolved_options.plan:
   339→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   340→            all_items,
   341→            resolved_options.plan,
   342→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   343→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   344→        )
   345→
   346→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   347→    should_collapse = (
   348→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   349→        and resolved_options.plan
   350→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   351→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   352→    )
   353→    if should_collapse:
   354→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   355→
   356→    should_interleave = (
   357→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   358→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   359→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   360→        and resolved_options.tier is None
   361→        and not resolved_options.scope
   362→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   363→    )
   364→    if should_interleave:
   365→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   366→
   367→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   368→
   369→    requested_tier = (
   370→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   371→    )
   372→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   373→    fallback_reason = None
   374→    filtered = all_items
   375→
   376→    if requested_tier is not None:
   377→        filtered = [
   378→            item
   379→            for item in all_items
   380→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   381→        ]
   382→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   383→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   384→            if chosen is not None:
   385→                selected_tier = chosen
   386→                filtered = [
   387→                    item
   388→                    for item in all_items
   389→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   390→                ]
   391→                fallback_reason = (
   392→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   393→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   394→                )
   395→        elif not filtered:
   396→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   397→
   398→    total = len(filtered)
   399→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   400→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   401→
   402→    if resolved_options.explain:
   403→        for item in filtered:
   404→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   405→
   406→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   407→    return {
   408→        "items": filtered,
   409→        "total": total,
   410→        "tier_counts": counts,
   411→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   412→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   413→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   414→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   415→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   416→    }
   417→
   418→
   419→__all__ = [
   420→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   421→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   422→    "WorkQueueResult",
   423→    "build_work_queue",
   424→    "group_queue_items",
   425→]
   426→

This is a key finding. The work queue core (`build_work_queue`) builds items from `build_finding_items` and `build_subjective_items`, but never calls `build_synthesis_item`. The `synthesis::pending` ID is injected into `plan["queue_order"]` but the `_apply_plan_order` function only maps IDs to existing items in `by_id` -- and since there's no work item built for `synthesis::pending`, it will simply be silently skipped when applying plan order. The synthesis item would never surface in `next` output.

Let me verify this is the actual behavior.

Confirmed: line 146 checks `if fid in by_id` -- since `synthesis::pending` has no matching item in `by_id` (no item was built for it), it's silently dropped. The `build_synthesis_item` function exists to create such an item, but it's never called in the `build_work_queue` pipeline.

## Issues Found

### Issue 1 (Moderate): `build_synthesis_item` is dead code in production

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` (line 171)

`build_synthesis_item()` creates a synthetic T1 work item for `synthesis::pending`, but it is:
- Not in `__all__` of `helpers.py`
- Not re-exported through `work_queue.py`
- Not called anywhere in `core.py`'s `build_work_queue` pipeline
- Only called from test code (`test_epic_synthesis.py`)

This means that when `synthesis::pending` is injected into `plan["queue_order"]`, the `_apply_plan_order` function in `core.py` (line 146) silently drops it because there's no matching item in `by_id`. **The synthesis::pending item will never surface in `next` output** via the normal work queue path.

The `synthesis_phase_banner()` function exists to print a banner instead, but it's also not wired into any command (see Issue 2).

### Issue 2 (Moderate): `print_synthesis_guardrail_banner` is dead code

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/guardrails.py`

This function is defined and properly imports `synthesis_phase_banner`, but it is **never called** from any command entrypoint (`next`, `fix`, `show`, `status`, etc.). The intent was presumably to alert users that synthesis is needed before they start working on individual findings, but the wiring was never completed.

### Issue 3 (Minor): `build_synthesis_item` missing from `__all__`

Even if Issue 1 is resolved by wiring `build_synthesis_item` into the work queue, it should be added to `helpers.py`'s `__all__` list.

---

## Summary

The synthesis implementation is **structurally complete** -- all modules exist, all imports resolve, all the schema/migration/persistence/handler/parser/dispatch pieces are wired correctly. The staged workflow (OBSERVE -> REFLECT -> ORGANIZE -> COMMIT) is fully implemented with proper validation gates.

The two significant gaps are both about **surfacing synthesis to the user through normal command flows**:

1. `synthesis::pending` never appears in `next` output because `build_synthesis_item` is never called in the work queue pipeline.
2. The guardrail banner that should warn users on `next`/`fix`/etc. is implemented but never wired into those commands.

These are the same gap viewed from two angles: the synthesis system correctly injects `synthesis::pending` into the plan queue and correctly removes it on completion, but there's no bridge between the plan queue's `synthesis::pending` marker and the user-facing work queue surfaces. The user would only discover synthesis is needed by running `desloppify plan synthesize` directly (the dashboard), or by seeing the scan workflow's post-scan message.
